- **mycelium**: I realized I let the agent start coding before I was sure it was working on the right thing, so I built this. Now it doesn't get to write code before there's a reason. The agent asks who this is for. It asks what you're assuming, and what would show you wrong. The rules run as hooks, so once you have started it in a repo the build stays shut until the answers are there. Purpose, strategy, opportunities, then delivery and market feedback. It adds skills and hooks, keeps its own state in a .claude/ folder, and won't edit the files already in your repo unless you ask it to. ([haabe/mycelium](https://github.com/haabe/mycelium/tree/HEAD/plugins/mycelium) · ★54 · 63 skills, 1 hook file)
